## A1 · Meet and make a plan

Unit reference: A1

### Learning goal

Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs.

Builds on: Start here

### Pattern explanation

Use mano vardas for a name, gyvenu for residence and man patinka for a liking. Vilniuje is the location form used here; it is not the form to copy into every place expression.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- Labas - hello [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-0.3be00b950d7c.mp3)
- vardas - name [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-1.14496226c2dc.mp3)
- gyvenu - I live [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-2.2cee5e35641f.mp3)
- Vilniuje - in Vilnius [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-3.51872f3f2e53.mp3)
- butas - flat [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-4.3b24de45369c.mp3)
- mažas - small (masculine singular) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-5.c967b1dd953a.mp3)
- muzika - music [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-6.906d6aa0806f.mp3)
- rytoj - tomorrow [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-7.d0cca6c75219.mp3)

### Additional source language

- aštuntą — at eight, with valandą understood
  - Aštuntą valandą.
- gal — perhaps / shall we, in the invitation
  - Gal susitikime rytoj aštuntą valandą?
- gyveni — you live, in kur gyveni?
  - Kur gyveni?
- iki — until / see you, in iki rytojaus
  - Iki rytojaus!
- koks — what, in koks tavo vardas?
  - Koks tavo vardas?
- kur — where
  - Kur gyveni?
- parke — in the park
  - Rytoj susitikime parke.
- Rasa — a person’s name
  - Mano vardas Rasa.
- rytojaus — tomorrow, in the complete farewell iki rytojaus
  - Iki rytojaus!
- tavo — your, addressing one person informally
  - Koks tavo vardas?
- taip — yes, as an affirmative response
  - Taip, iki rytojaus!

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Labas! — Hello!
- Mano vardas Lina. — My name is Lina.
- Aš esu iš Lietuvos. — I am from Lithuania.
- Aš gyvenu Vilniuje. — I live in Vilnius.
- Man patinka muzika. — I like music.
- Susitikime rytoj. — Let’s meet tomorrow.
- Septintą valandą. — At seven o’clock.
- Mano butas mažas. — My flat is small.

### Teaching notes

- Use a short sentence for each fact. The reader should be able to find your name, place or plan easily.
- A name or place can change without changing the useful sentence pattern. Start from what you know.
- Read your message aloud, then check whether the person receiving it has the information they need.

### Reading source

Labas! Mano vardas Lina. Aš esu iš Lietuvos. Aš gyvenu Vilniuje. Mano butas mažas. Man patinka muzika. Susitikime rytoj. Septintą valandą.

English guide:
Hello! My name is Lina. I am from Lithuania. I live in Vilnius. My flat is small. I like music. Let us meet tomorrow, at seven.

### Listening source

Labas! Mano vardas Rasa. Gyvenu Vilniuje. Man patinka muzika. Rytoj susitikime parke. Aštuntą valandą. Iki rytojaus!

English guide:
Hello! My name is Rasa/Rosa. I live in Vilnius/Mexico City. I like music. Let us meet tomorrow in the park, at eight. See you tomorrow!

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.listen.c82c8a27a301.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. What is the main purpose?
   - Introduce someone and arrange a meeting **[key]**
   - Complain about a hotel
2. When is the meeting?
   - Tomorrow at seven **[key]**
   - Today at eight
3. Which detail describes the writer’s home?
   - It is small **[key]**
   - It has a garden
   - It is near a station

### Listening task key

1. When is the listening meeting?
   - Tomorrow at eight **[key]**
   - Tomorrow at seven
   - Today at eight
2. Where will they meet?
   - At home
   - In the park **[key]**
   - At the station
3. What interest does the speaker mention?
   - Cooking
   - Football
   - Music **[key]**

### Spoken production

Introduce a real or invented person, say where they live and mention an interest. Then invite your listener to meet and give a time.

- Give a name and a place.
- State an interest.
- Make the day and time clear.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. Labas! Koks tavo vardas?
   - Meaning: Hello! What is your name?
   - Response goal: Introduce yourself.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.turn-0.bae4bbb99041.mp3)
2. Kur gyveni?
   - Meaning: Where do you live?
   - Response goal: Say where you live.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.turn-1.ad8fd9599339.mp3)
3. Gal susitikime rytoj aštuntą valandą?
   - Meaning: Shall we meet tomorrow at eight?
   - Response goal: Accept the invitation or propose another time.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.turn-2.e0e686ff69a3.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Tell a friend how the reading and listening meeting plans differ. Make both meeting times clear.

### Writing assignments

#### Introduce yourself

Reader: A new member of your language group

Write a short introduction. Use your own details or an invented profile.

Suggested length: 8–35 words.

- Give a name.
- Say where you live or are from.
- Mention one interest.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Mano vardas Rasa. Aš esu iš Lietuvos. Man patinka muzika.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Give a name
  - Mano vardas Rasa.
  - This identifies the person writing.
- Say where you live or are from
  - Aš esu iš Lietuvos.
  - This supplies origin; a residence would also meet this goal.
- Mention an interest
  - Man patinka muzika.
  - This gives the new group member something to talk about.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### A note about home

Reader: A friend who is visiting

Write a brief note describing your home and inviting your friend to meet.

Suggested length: 6–30 words.

- Give one simple fact about your home.
- Suggest meeting tomorrow.
- Include a meeting time.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Mano butas mažas. Susitikime rytoj. Aštuntą valandą.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Give a simple fact about home
  - Mano butas mažas.
  - This gives the visitor one useful description.
- Suggest meeting tomorrow
  - Susitikime rytoj.
  - This proposes the meeting day.
- Give the meeting time
  - Aštuntą valandą.
  - This makes the arrangement usable; a day alone is incomplete.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### Arrange a first meeting

Reader: Someone you have just met

Write a friendly message arranging to meet.

Suggested length: 8–30 words.

- Begin with a greeting.
- Introduce yourself briefly.
- Give a day and a time.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Labas! Mano vardas Lina. Susitikime rytoj. Septintą valandą.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Greet and introduce yourself
  - Labas! Mano vardas Lina.
  - The reader can identify who is contacting them.
- Give a day
  - Susitikime rytoj.
  - This supplies tomorrow as the meeting day.
- Give a time
  - Septintą valandą.
  - This supplies the time without changing the day.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

### Required practical table reading

A table gives meaning through both its headings and the position of each cell. Find the requested column before following its row.
The row labels distinguish original details from the changed or requested details. A proposed replacement is not proof that a real booking has been confirmed.
The second task turns the table: field labels run down the side. Compare the table with the displayed message before selecting an answer.
- Original details: Vardas = Lina; Miestas = Vilnius; Susitikimo laikas = septintą valandą
- Changed / requested details: Vardas = Rasa; Miestas = Vilnius; Susitikimo laikas = aštuntą valandą

#### Read across the rows

Layout: rows

Displayed comparison source:
Labas! Mano vardas Lina. Aš esu iš Lietuvos. Aš gyvenu Vilniuje. Mano butas mažas. Man patinka muzika. Susitikime rytoj. Septintą valandą.

| Source details | Vardas | Miestas | Susitikimo laikas |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Original details | Lina | Vilnius | septintą valandą |
| Changed / requested details | Rasa | Vilnius | aštuntą valandą |

- In the original details, what belongs under Vardas?
  - Lina **[key]**
  - Rasa
  - Vilnius
  - septintą valandą
  - Explanation: Vardas means name. Follow that column to the original details row.
- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Susitikimo laikas?
  - aštuntą valandą **[key]**
  - septintą valandą
  - Lina
  - Vilnius
  - Explanation: Susitikimo laikas means meeting time. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- Compare the two rows. Which details change?
  - Name + Meeting time **[key]**
  - Name
  - City
  - Meeting time
  - None of the details
  - Explanation: Changed fields: Name + Meeting time. Unchanged: City.

#### Compare a message with the table

Layout: columns

Displayed comparison source:
Labas! Mano vardas Rasa. Gyvenu Vilniuje. Man patinka muzika. Rytoj susitikime parke. Aštuntą valandą. Iki rytojaus!

| Field | Original details | Changed / requested details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Vardas | Lina | Rasa |
| Miestas | Vilnius | Vilnius |
| Susitikimo laikas | septintą valandą | aštuntą valandą |

- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Vardas?
  - Rasa **[key]**
  - Lina
  - Vilnius
  - septintą valandą
  - Explanation: Vardas means name. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- In the original details, what belongs under Miestas?
  - Vilnius **[key]**
  - Lina
  - septintą valandą
  - Explanation: Miestas means city. Follow that column to the original details row.
- Which table column matches the displayed changed / requested message?
  - Original details
  - Changed / requested details **[key]**
  - Explanation: Use the changed / requested details column. The other column records the original source.

### Required practical forms

The labels and field formats are explicitly taught. Both prepared-source forms are required; detail checking is not an open-writing accuracy grade.
Vilniuje means in Vilnius. A city-name field uses Vilnius. Keep the meeting time from the selected source.

Label preparation:
- Vardas — Name; original: Lina; changed: Rasa
- Miestas — City; original: Vilnius; changed: Vilnius
- Susitikimo laikas — Meeting time; original: septintą valandą; changed: aštuntą valandą

- Susitikimo laikas: aštuntą valandą

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A1:confirm

Question purpose: At eight?

Prepared question: Aštuntą valandą?

Partner message: Aštuntą valandą.

Detail check: Which time did your partner clarify?

Key: Eight

Optional response frame: Aštuntą valandą.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified time so your partner can hear you understood.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1:correct

Question purpose: At seven?

Prepared question: Septintą valandą?

Partner message: Rytoj. Aštuntą valandą.

Detail check: Which time did your partner clarify?

Key: Eight

Optional response frame: Aštuntą valandą.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified time so your partner can hear you understood.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask a short question to check the meeting time, then respond to the clarified time.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

<a id="a1-shopping"></a>
## A1 · Buy everyday things

Unit reference: A1-shopping

### Learning goal

Understand an item, quantity and price, and make a short purchase request.

Builds on: A1

### Pattern explanation

Learn quantities with their complete noun forms: du obuoliai names two apples. Norėčiau duonos is the request pattern here; do not substitute any noun ending without checking its pattern.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- parduotuvė - shop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-0.fd377bdb2a1e.mp3)
- obuoliai - apples [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-1.66c36a058f7c.mp3)
- du - two [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-2.ea58123c3ff7.mp3)
- trys - three [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-3.82274738dafd.mp3)
- duonos - bread (request form) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-4.9cdc3c4e90c6.mp3)
- kainuoja - costs [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-5.90adfb76bc6b.mp3)
- kortele - by card [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-6.034762d66335.mp3)
- grynaisiais - in cash [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-7.2b614077ee04.mp3)

### Additional source language

- ar — introduces a yes/no question
  - Ar norėtumėte dar ko nors?
- dar — more / else, in ar dar ko nors?
  - Ar norėtumėte dar ko nors?
- diena — day, in the greeting laba diena
  - Laba diena!
- eurus — euros, object form in a price expression
  - Obuoliai kainuoja tris eurus.
- gerai — okay / well
  - Gerai.
- ir — and
  - Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos.
- keturi — four, in the stated price
  - Keturi eurai?
- ko — part of ko nors: anything / something
  - Ko norėtumėte?
- laba — part of the greeting laba diena: hello / good day
  - Laba diena!
- mokėsite — you will pay, polite or plural
  - Mokėsite kortele ar grynaisiais?
- nors — part of ko nors: anything / something
  - Ar norėtumėte dar ko nors?
- norėtumėte — you would like, polite or plural
  - Ko norėtumėte?
- obuolius — apples, object form after perku here
  - Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos.
- obuolių — apples, form used with the requested amount here
  - Norėčiau trijų obuolių.
- parduotuvėje — in the shop
  - Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos.
- perku — I buy / am buying
  - Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos.
- trijų — three, form used in the request norėčiau trijų obuolių
  - Norėčiau trijų obuolių.
- tris — three, object form in tris eurus
  - Obuoliai kainuoja tris eurus.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- parduotuvė — shop
- obuoliai — apples
- du obuoliai — two apples
- trys eurai — three euros / thirty pesos
- Norėčiau duonos. — I would like bread.
- Kiek kainuoja? — How much does it cost?
- Mokėsiu kortele. — I will pay by card.
- Ačiū, viso gero! — Thank you, goodbye!

### Teaching notes

- Say the item and quantity clearly. A price and an item count answer different questions.
- Compare the Lithuanian forms: du obuoliai names the items; perku du obuolius uses the object form, and norėčiau trijų obuolių expresses the requested amount. Learn each complete pattern.
- The Lithuanian case uses euros; the Mexican Spanish case uses pesos. Keep each source’s amount separate.

### Reading source

Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos. Obuoliai kainuoja tris eurus. Mokėsiu kortele. Ačiū, viso gero!

English guide:
At the shop I buy two apples and bread. The apples cost three euros / thirty pesos. I will pay by card. Thank you, goodbye!

### Listening source

Laba diena! Norėčiau trijų obuolių. Kiek kainuoja? Keturi eurai? Gerai. Mokėsiu grynaisiais. Ačiū!

English guide:
The customer asks for three apples, confirms a price of four euros / forty pesos, and pays in cash.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.listen.785e81649c51.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. How many apples are bought?
   - Two **[key]**
   - Three
   - Four
2. How will the customer pay?
   - In cash
   - By card **[key]**
   - With a voucher
3. What other item is bought?
   - Milk
   - Tea
   - Bread **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. How many apples are requested?
   - Two
   - Three **[key]**
   - Four
2. How will this customer pay?
   - In cash **[key]**
   - By card
   - The friend will pay
3. Which amount is confirmed?
   - Three euros
   - Ten euros
   - Four euros **[key]**

### Spoken production

Describe a small shopping trip, including the items, one quantity and your payment method.

- Name the items.
- Keep quantities and prices distinct.
- Make the payment method clear.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. Laba diena! Ko norėtumėte?
   - Meaning: Hello! What would you like?
   - Response goal: Request an item and give a quantity.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.turn-0.84e17d0d4652.mp3)
2. Ar norėtumėte dar ko nors?
   - Meaning: Would you like anything else?
   - Response goal: Add one item or politely say that is all.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.turn-1.127296a0556d.mp3)
3. Mokėsite kortele ar grynaisiais?
   - Meaning: Will you pay by card or in cash?
   - Response goal: State your payment method and close politely.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.turn-2.fe08154c5a39.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Tell a friend the difference between the two customers’ purchases and payment methods.

### Writing assignments

#### A shopping message

Reader: A friend buying things for you

Write a short message asking your friend to buy everyday items. Use real or invented details.

Suggested length: 6–25 words.

- Name two items.
- Give at least one quantity.
- Add a polite closing.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Du obuoliai. Norėčiau duonos. Ačiū, viso gero!

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Name an item and quantity
  - Du obuoliai.
  - A short shopping note can list an item and its quantity without a full request sentence.
- Name a different item
  - Norėčiau duonos.
  - The friend needs two different items; repeating apples twice does not add a second item.
- Close politely
  - Ačiū, viso gero!
  - This closes the short note politely.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A short shopping note: item, price and planned payment

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### two-four-card

Du obuoliai. Keturi eurai. Mokėsiu kortele.

- How many apples are listed?
  - Model detail: Du obuoliai.
- What is the total price in euros?
  - Model detail: Keturi eurai.
- How will the shopper pay?
  - Model detail: Kortele.

#### three-three-cash

Trys obuoliai. Trys eurai. Mokėsiu grynaisiais.

- How many apples are listed?
  - Model detail: Trys obuoliai.
- What is the total price in euros?
  - Model detail: Trys eurai.
- How will the shopper pay?
  - Model detail: Grynaisiais.

### Controlled listening transfer

A shopper says the quantity, price and payment method

- How many apples does the shopper want?
  - Model detail: Trys obuoliai.

  - Model detail: Keturi eurai.

  - Model detail: Kortele.

Laba diena! Du obuoliai. Trys eurai. Mokėsiu grynaisiais. Ačiū, viso gero!

  - Model detail: Du obuoliai.
- What is the total price in euros?
  - Model detail: Trys eurai.
- How will the shopper pay?
  - Model detail: Grynaisiais.

- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

<a id="a1-home"></a>
## A1 · Welcome someone to your home

Unit reference: A1-home

### Learning goal

Understand simple room descriptions and give a visitor useful information.

Builds on: A1-shopping

### Pattern explanation

Kambarys šviesus and namai maži use different agreement forms. Čia yra locates something; ant stalo means on the table in this example.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- namai - home [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-0.8f473208c01a.mp3)
- virtuvė - kitchen [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-1.7ca4f232bdc4.mp3)
- kambarys - room [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-2.dbfc70b377c7.mp3)
- vonios kambarys - bathroom [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-3.dec3c2566b9b.mp3)
- raktas - key [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-4.a6c1367906e8.mp3)
- stalo - table (form in ant stalo) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-5.8c0ae4afc780.mp3)
- kėdės - chair (form in ant kėdės) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-6.b085c62e1a24.mp3)
- vandens - water (request form) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.word-7.a718f1d05a90.mp3)

### Additional source language

- antrame — second, location form in antrame aukšte
  - Mano butas yra antrame aukšte.
- aukšte — on the floor / storey, in antrame aukšte
  - Mano butas yra antrame aukšte.
- bet — but
  - Mano namai maži, bet šviesūs.
- didelis — big / large, masculine singular
  - Šis kambarys didelis, o virtuvė maža.
- gal — perhaps / could, in the request
  - Gal nori vandens?
- galiu — I can
  - Kur galiu padėti savo krepšį?
- gauti — get / receive
  - Ar galiu gauti vandens?
- krepšį — bag, object form
  - Kur galiu padėti savo krepšį?
- kur — where
  - Kur yra virtuvė?
- maža — small, feminine singular
  - Šis kambarys didelis, o virtuvė maža.
- o — and / while, linking contrasting details
  - Šis kambarys didelis, o virtuvė maža.
- padėti — put / place, in the question about the bag
  - Kur galiu padėti savo krepšį?
- savo — one’s own, referring back to the person involved
  - Kur galiu padėti savo krepšį?
- tavo — your, informal singular
  - Tavo raktas yra ant kėdės.
- užeik — come in, informal singular
  - Užeik.
- virtuvės — kitchen, form used after šalia
  - Vonios kambarys yra šalia virtuvės.
- šalia — beside / next to
  - Vonios kambarys yra šalia virtuvės.
- šis — this, masculine singular
  - Šis kambarys didelis, o virtuvė maža.
- šviesūs — bright, masculine plural with namai
  - Mano namai maži, bet šviesūs.

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Mano namai maži. — My home is small.
- Čia yra virtuvė. — Here is the kitchen.
- Kambarys šviesus. — The room is bright.
- Vonios kambarys yra čia. — The bathroom is here.
- Prašau užeiti. — Please come in.
- Atsisėsk čia. — Sit here.
- Ar nori arbatos? — Would you like tea?
- Raktas yra ant stalo. — The key is on the table.

### Teaching notes

- Use a short sentence to locate each room or object.
- Compare agreement: kambarys šviesus is singular; namai šviesūs is plural. The location pattern šalia virtuvės means beside the kitchen.
- For a visitor, useful information is more important than listing every object in the home.

### Reading source

Labas! Prašau užeiti. Mano namai maži, bet šviesūs. Čia yra virtuvė. Vonios kambarys yra šalia virtuvės. Raktas yra ant stalo. Ar nori arbatos?

English guide:
The host welcomes a visitor to a small, bright home. The bathroom is beside the kitchen. The key is on the table, and the host offers tea.

### Listening source

Labas! Mano butas yra antrame aukšte. Užeik. Šis kambarys didelis, o virtuvė maža. Tavo raktas yra ant kėdės. Gal nori vandens?

English guide:
The flat is on the second floor. The room is large and the kitchen small. The visitor’s key is on the chair, and the host offers water.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.listen.55ab4f8645e8.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. Where is the bathroom?
   - Beside the kitchen **[key]**
   - Outside the building
   - Beside the bedroom
2. Where is the key?
   - On the chair
   - On the table **[key]**
   - In a bag
3. What drink is offered?
   - Coffee
   - Water
   - Tea **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. Which floor is mentioned?
   - The first
   - The second **[key]**
   - The third
2. Where is this key?
   - On the chair **[key]**
   - On the table
   - By the door
3. How is the kitchen described?
   - Large
   - Dark
   - Small **[key]**

### Spoken production

Welcome a visitor and describe a real or invented home.

- Describe one room.
- Locate a useful object.
- Make a simple offer to the visitor.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. Kur yra virtuvė?
   - Meaning: Where is the kitchen?
   - Response goal: Tell your visitor where the kitchen is.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.turn-0.f1bdc3dbb158.mp3)
2. Kur galiu padėti savo krepšį?
   - Meaning: Where can I put my bag?
   - Response goal: Suggest a simple place for the bag.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.turn-1.e5cc76f73e8b.mp3)
3. Ar galiu gauti vandens?
   - Meaning: Could I have water?
   - Response goal: Respond politely to the visitor’s request.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-home.turn-2.7766406a57ca.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Leave a voice note telling the visitor where to find the key in each of the two homes. Do not mix up the table and chair.

### Writing assignments

#### A note for a visitor

Reader: A friend arriving at your home

Write a useful arrival note for a real or invented home.

Suggested length: 8–35 words.

- Welcome the visitor.
- Describe where to find a room or object.
- Give one useful instruction or offer.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Prašau užeiti. Raktas yra ant kėdės. Ar nori arbatos?

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Welcome the visitor
  - Prašau užeiti.
  - This tells the visitor they can come in.
- Locate a useful object
  - Raktas yra ant kėdės.
  - This tells the visitor where to find the key.
- Make a simple offer
  - Ar nori arbatos?
  - This offers a drink; an appropriate useful instruction could also meet this goal.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A note for someone visiting a home

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

<a id="a1-travel"></a>
## A1 · Find a stop and buy a ticket

Unit reference: A1-travel

### Learning goal

Understand simple directions, a departure time and a ticket request.

Builds on: A1-home

### Pattern explanation

Eikite and sukite are polite direction forms here. Į dešinę and į kairę must stay distinct. Vieną bilietą is the complete object phrase in the ticket request.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- stotelė - bus stop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-0.7c0f5e772f0f.mp3)
- autobusas - bus [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-1.c1689ddc01ab.mp3)
- tiesiai - straight ahead [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-2.297cec7d2ee9.mp3)
- dešinę - right (direction) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-3.179c5f435edc.mp3)
- kairę - left (direction) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-4.15082cbfb211.mp3)
- bilietą - ticket (object form) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-5.428bea5ef0c6.mp3)
- atvažiuoja - arrives [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-6.ad3914826ebd.mp3)
- vienuoliktą - at eleven (time form) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-7.f8b9a77a0f2c.mp3)

### Additional source language

- autobuse — on the bus
  - Bilietą pirkite autobuse.
- bilietų — tickets, form after kiek
  - Kiek bilietų norėtumėte?
- galite — you can, polite or plural
  - Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.
- ir — and
  - Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę.
- norėtumėte — you would like, polite or plural
  - Kur norėtumėte nuvažiuoti?
- nusipirkti — buy / purchase for oneself
  - Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.
- nuvažiuoti — travel / get to a place by transport
  - Kur norėtumėte nuvažiuoti?
- parduotuvės — shop, form used after prie
  - Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės.
- parko — park, form used after prie
  - Stotelė yra prie parko.
- paskui — then / afterwards
  - Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę.
- pirkite — buy, polite or plural instruction
  - Bilietą pirkite autobuse.
- prie — by / near, with the following place form
  - Stotelė yra prie parko.
- stotį — station, destination form after į
  - Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą.
- žinote — you know, polite or plural
  - Ar žinote, kur yra stotelė?

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- stotelė — bus stop
- stotis — station / bus terminal
- Eikite tiesiai. — Go straight.
- Sukite į dešinę. — Turn right.
- Sukite į kairę. — Turn left.
- Kur yra stotelė? — Where is the stop?
- Vieną bilietą, prašau. — One ticket, please.
- Autobusas atvažiuoja dešimtą. — The bus arrives at ten.

### Teaching notes

- Separate the walking directions from the bus destination.
- The examples use polite directions: eikite / siga, sukite / dé vuelta. Follow the complete pattern.
- A stop and a terminal are different places. Check the place, direction and time.

### Reading source

Stotelė yra prie parko. Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą. Bilietą pirkite autobuse.

English guide:
The stop is beside the park. Go straight and turn right. The bus to the terminal arrives at ten. Buy the ticket on the bus.

### Listening source

Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja vienuoliktą. Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.

English guide:
This stop is beside the shop. Go straight, then turn left. The terminal bus arrives at eleven. A ticket can be bought on the bus.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.listen.c073a6163a4f.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. What is beside the stop?
   - The park **[key]**
   - The shop
   - The library
2. Which turn is given?
   - Left
   - Right **[key]**
   - No turn
3. When does the bus arrive?
   - Eleven
   - Nine
   - Ten **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. Which turn is given in the recording?
   - Right
   - Left **[key]**
   - No turn
2. Where is this stop?
   - Beside the shop **[key]**
   - Inside the terminal
   - Beside the library
3. What time is mentioned?
   - Ten
   - Twelve
   - Eleven **[key]**

### Spoken production

Explain how a visitor can find a stop and catch a bus.

- Locate the stop.
- Give a direction.
- State a time and ticket detail.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. Kur norėtumėte nuvažiuoti?
   - Meaning: Where would you like to go?
   - Response goal: State a simple destination.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.turn-0.1030684bdc10.mp3)
2. Ar žinote, kur yra stotelė?
   - Meaning: Do you know where the stop is?
   - Response goal: Ask for the stop or check a direction.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.turn-1.7c5106e036dd.mp3)
3. Kiek bilietų norėtumėte?
   - Meaning: How many tickets would you like?
   - Response goal: Request a quantity and close politely.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.turn-2.991c9220beaa.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Tell a visitor how the written and spoken routes differ, especially the turning direction and bus time.

### Writing assignments

#### A short travel note

Reader: A visiting friend

Write simple directions to a real or invented stop, with a useful bus detail.

Suggested length: 12–40 words.

- Locate the stop.
- Give a clear direction.
- Include a time or ticket instruction.


##### Project-specific preparation

Use the information your reader needs. A short, complete message is fine; the suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai. Sukite į dešinę. Autobusas atvažiuoja vienuoliktą.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Locate the stop
  - Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės.
  - The landmark identifies where the visitor should go.
- Give a direction
  - Eikite tiesiai. Sukite į dešinę.
  - This gives the walking sequence. Right and left must stay distinct.
- Give a useful bus detail
  - Autobusas atvažiuoja vienuoliktą.
  - The arrival time adds a practical transport detail; a learned ticket instruction is another valid choice.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

Directions from a local person

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### park-right-eleven

Stotelė yra prie parko. Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja vienuoliktą. Bilietą pirkite autobuse.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Prie parko.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: Į dešinę.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: Vienuoliktą.

#### shop-left-ten

Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą. Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Prie parduotuvės.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: Į kairę.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: Dešimtą.

### Controlled listening transfer

A local person gives directions and a bus time

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### spoken-1

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/lt.A1-travel.spoken-1.abd57e940aed.mp3)

Stotelė yra prie parko. Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą. Bilietą pirkite autobuse.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Prie parko.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: Į kairę.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: Dešimtą.

#### spoken-2

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/lt.A1-travel.spoken-2.3efed754e985.mp3)

Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja vienuoliktą. Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.

- What is the stop near?
  - Model detail: Prie parduotuvės.
- Which way should you turn?
  - Model detail: Į dešinę.
- At what time does the bus arrive?
  - Model detail: Vienuoliktą.

### Required practical table reading

A table gives meaning through both its headings and the position of each cell. Find the requested column before following its row.
The row labels distinguish original details from the changed or requested details. A proposed replacement is not proof that a real booking has been confirmed.
The second task turns the table: field labels run down the side. Compare the table with the displayed message before selecting an answer.
- Original details: Stotelės vieta = prie parko; Posūkis = į dešinę; Atvykimo laikas = dešimtą
- Changed / requested details: Stotelės vieta = prie parduotuvės; Posūkis = į kairę; Atvykimo laikas = vienuoliktą

#### Read across the rows

Layout: rows

Displayed comparison source:
Stotelė yra prie parko. Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą. Bilietą pirkite autobuse.

| Source details | Stotelės vieta | Posūkis | Atvykimo laikas |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Original details | prie parko | į dešinę | dešimtą |
| Changed / requested details | prie parduotuvės | į kairę | vienuoliktą |

- In the original details, what belongs under Stotelės vieta?
  - prie parko **[key]**
  - prie parduotuvės
  - į dešinę
  - dešimtą
  - Explanation: Stotelės vieta means stop location. Follow that column to the original details row.
- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Atvykimo laikas?
  - vienuoliktą **[key]**
  - dešimtą
  - prie parko
  - į dešinę
  - Explanation: Atvykimo laikas means arrival time. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- Compare the two rows. Which details change?
  - Stop location + Turn + Arrival time **[key]**
  - Stop location
  - Turn
  - Arrival time
  - None of the details
  - Explanation: Changed fields: Stop location + Turn + Arrival time. All three requested details differ.

#### Compare a message with the table

Layout: columns

Displayed comparison source:
Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja vienuoliktą. Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.

| Field | Original details | Changed / requested details |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Stotelės vieta | prie parko | prie parduotuvės |
| Posūkis | į dešinę | į kairę |
| Atvykimo laikas | dešimtą | vienuoliktą |

- In the changed / requested details, what belongs under Stotelės vieta?
  - prie parduotuvės **[key]**
  - prie parko
  - į dešinę
  - dešimtą
  - Explanation: Stotelės vieta means stop location. Follow that column to the changed / requested details row.
- In the original details, what belongs under Posūkis?
  - į dešinę **[key]**
  - į kairę
  - prie parko
  - dešimtą
  - Explanation: Posūkis means turn. Follow that column to the original details row.
- Which table column matches the displayed changed / requested message?
  - Original details
  - Changed / requested details **[key]**
  - Explanation: Use the changed / requested details column. The other column records the original source.

### Required practical forms

The labels and field formats are explicitly taught. Both prepared-source forms are required; detail checking is not an open-writing accuracy grade.
Keep the complete location and direction chunks: prie parko / prie parduotuvės and į dešinę / į kairę. Dešimtą and vienuoliktą give the arrival time.

Label preparation:
- Stotelės vieta — Stop location; original: prie parko; changed: prie parduotuvės
- Posūkis — Turn; original: į dešinę; changed: į kairę
- Atvykimo laikas — Arrival time; original: dešimtą; changed: vienuoliktą

#### Record the original details

Source: prepared reading

Stotelė yra prie parko. Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą. Bilietą pirkite autobuse.

Field key:
- Stotelės vieta: prie parko
- Posūkis: į dešinę
- Atvykimo laikas: dešimtą

#### Record the changed source details

Source: prepared message shown as text

Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja vienuoliktą. Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.

Field key:
- Stotelės vieta: prie parduotuvės
- Posūkis: į kairę
- Atvykimo laikas: vienuoliktą

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A1-travel:confirm

Question purpose: To the left?

Prepared question: Į kairę?

Partner message: Sukite į kairę.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Sukite į kairę.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-travel:correct

Question purpose: To the right?

Prepared question: Į dešinę?

Partner message: Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Sukite į kairę.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided prepared-pattern feedback

Local pattern recognition supports revision; unmatched wording can be valid. No grammar, pronunciation or proficiency score is assigned. Speaking feedback checks optional notes typed after a real recording and replay, not the audio itself.

#### A1-travel.writing

- Locate the stop
  - Prepared example: Stotelė yra prie parko.
- Give a direction
  - Prepared example: Eikite tiesiai.
- Include a bus time or ticket detail
  - Prepared example: Autobusas atvažiuoja dešimtą.

#### A1-travel.production

- Locate the stop
  - Prepared example: Stotelė yra prie parko.
- Give a direction
  - Prepared example: Eikite tiesiai.
- Include a bus time or ticket detail
  - Prepared example: Autobusas atvažiuoja dešimtą.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask a short question to check a turn, then respond to the clarified direction.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: Į kairę?
  - Meaning: To the left?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A1-travel.question-confirm.6987722281bf.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Sukite į kairę.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A1-travel.confirm.d48bcd395045.mp3)

- Checking question: Į dešinę?
  - Meaning: To the right?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A1-travel.question-correct.acced669917d.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A1-travel.correct.b477e497d405.mp3)

Partner-detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Own-response goal: Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.

Prepared wording: Sukite į kairę.

<a id="a1-capstone"></a>
## A1 · Two useful messages

Unit reference: A1-capstone

### Learning goal

Write two short messages: one about the purchase and one about reaching the stop. Keep the two requests separate.

Builds on: A1-travel

### Pattern explanation

Give one useful detail at a time. A polite request can close a brief practical message.

### Support plan

Start with the meaning guide and complete patterns. Practise one fact at a time; then cover the guide and try again.

### Skill targets

- reading: Find explicit personal details and practical information in a short, simple text. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Identify familiar people, places and times in a short, clear recording. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Give short, understandable statements about familiar people and needs. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Respond to simple questions and requests with preparation and repetition available. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on simple details from a short message without changing them. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Write short messages containing useful personal or practical details. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- parduotuvė - shop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-0.fd377bdb2a1e.mp3)
- obuoliai - apples [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-1.66c36a058f7c.mp3)
- du - two [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-2.ea58123c3ff7.mp3)
- trys - three [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.word-3.82274738dafd.mp3)
- stotelė - bus stop [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-0.7c0f5e772f0f.mp3)
- autobusas - bus [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-1.c1689ddc01ab.mp3)
- tiesiai - straight ahead [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-2.297cec7d2ee9.mp3)
- dešinę - right (direction) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.word-3.179c5f435edc.mp3)

### Additional source language

- ar — introduces a yes/no question
  - Ar norėtumėte dar ko nors?
- diena — day, in the greeting laba diena
  - Laba diena!
- eurus — euros, object form in a price expression
  - Obuoliai kainuoja tris eurus.
- ir — and
  - Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę.
- ko — part of ko nors: anything / something
  - Ko norėtumėte?
- norėtumėte — you would like, polite or plural
  - Kur norėtumėte nuvažiuoti?
- obuolius — apples, object form after perku here
  - Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos.
- obuolių — apples, form used with the requested amount here
  - Norėčiau trijų obuolių.
- perku — I buy / am buying
  - Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos.
- trijų — three, form used in the request norėčiau trijų obuolių
  - Norėčiau trijų obuolių.
- tris — three, object form in tris eurus
  - Obuoliai kainuoja tris eurus.
- autobuse — on the bus
  - Bilietą pirkite autobuse.
- bilietų — tickets, form after kiek
  - Kiek bilietų norėtumėte?
- galite — you can, polite or plural
  - Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.
- nusipirkti — buy / purchase for oneself
  - Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.
- nuvažiuoti — travel / get to a place by transport
  - Kur norėtumėte nuvažiuoti?
- parduotuvės — shop, form used after prie
  - Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės.
- parko — park, form used after prie
  - Stotelė yra prie parko.
- paskui — then / afterwards
  - Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę.
- pirkite — buy, polite or plural instruction
  - Bilietą pirkite autobuse.
- prie — by / near, with the following place form
  - Stotelė yra prie parko.
- stotį — station, destination form after į
  - Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą.
- Prašau parašyti laiką. Ačiū! — Please write the time. Thank you!
  - Prašau parašyti laiką. Ačiū!

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- parduotuvė — shop
- obuoliai — apples
- du obuoliai — two apples
- trys eurai — three euros / thirty pesos
- stotelė — bus stop
- stotis — station / bus terminal
- Eikite tiesiai. — Go straight.
- Prašau parašyti laiką. Ačiū! — Please write the time. Thank you!

### Teaching notes

- Revisit the language and source guides from both preceding cases.
- Give one useful detail at a time. A polite request can close a brief practical message.
- Plan for the stated reader. Use the worked model to study purpose, then supply your own response rather than copying it.

### Reading source

Parduotuvėje perku du obuolius ir duonos. Obuoliai kainuoja tris eurus. Mokėsiu kortele. Ačiū, viso gero!

Stotelė yra prie parko. Eikite tiesiai ir sukite į dešinę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja dešimtą. Bilietą pirkite autobuse.

English guide:
First written source: At the shop I buy two apples and bread. The apples cost three euros / thirty pesos. I will pay by card. Thank you, goodbye!

Second written source: The stop is beside the park. Go straight and turn right. The bus to the terminal arrives at ten. Buy the ticket on the bus.

A useful synthesis pattern

Prašau parašyti laiką. Ačiū!

Please write the time. Thank you!

### Listening source

Laba diena! Norėčiau trijų obuolių. Kiek kainuoja? Keturi eurai? Gerai. Mokėsiu grynaisiais. Ačiū!

Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę. Autobusas į stotį atvažiuoja vienuoliktą. Vieną bilietą galite nusipirkti autobuse.

English guide:
First spoken source: The customer asks for three apples, confirms a price of four euros / forty pesos, and pays in cash.

Second spoken source: This stop is beside the shop. Go straight, then turn left. The terminal bus arrives at eleven. A ticket can be bought on the bus.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-capstone.listen.7199b7b72bd8.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. First written source: How many apples are bought?
   - Two **[key]**
   - Three
   - Four
2. Second written source: What is beside the stop?
   - The park **[key]**
   - The shop
   - The library
3. Second written source: When does the bus arrive?
   - Eleven
   - Nine
   - Ten **[key]**

### Listening task key

1. First spoken source: How many apples are requested?
   - Two
   - Three **[key]**
   - Four
2. Second spoken source: Which turn is given in the recording?
   - Right
   - Left **[key]**
   - No turn
3. Second spoken source: What time is mentioned?
   - Ten
   - Twelve
   - Eleven **[key]**

### Spoken production

Write two short messages: one about the purchase and one about reaching the stop. Keep the two requests separate.

- Give the useful purchase details.
- Give the useful travel details.
- Close politely or request a useful detail.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. Laba diena! Ko norėtumėte?
   - Meaning: Hello! What would you like?
   - Response goal: Request an item and give a quantity.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-shopping.turn-0.84e17d0d4652.mp3)
2. Kur norėtumėte nuvažiuoti?
   - Meaning: Where would you like to go?
   - Response goal: State a simple destination.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.turn-0.1030684bdc10.mp3)
3. Kiek bilietų norėtumėte?
   - Meaning: How many tickets would you like?
   - Response goal: Request a quantity and close politely.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1-travel.turn-2.991c9220beaa.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Explain the two cases to the specified reader. Preserve the differences between the written and spoken versions, and between the two situations. Do not invent a shared person, date, budget or decision.

### Writing assignments

#### Two useful messages · written response

Reader: A friend buying something and finding a bus stop

Write two short messages: one about the purchase and one about reaching the stop. Keep the two requests separate.

Suggested length: 20–65 words.

- Give the useful purchase details.
- Give the useful travel details.
- Close politely or request a useful detail.


##### Project-specific preparation

Study how the two cases remain separate and how the final sentence serves the reader. Plan, draft and revise your own response. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR threshold.

Worked model:
Du obuoliai. Norėčiau duonos. Ačiū, viso gero!

Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai. Sukite į dešinę. Autobusas atvažiuoja vienuoliktą.

Prašau parašyti laiką. Ačiū!

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Give the useful purchase details.
  - Du obuoliai. Norėčiau duonos. Ačiū, viso gero!
  - The first case keeps its own details and qualifications. These model details come from the prepared unit.
- Give the useful travel details.
  - Stotelė yra prie parduotuvės. Eikite tiesiai. Sukite į dešinę. Autobusas atvažiuoja vienuoliktą.
  - The second case is presented separately, so its people, conditions and decisions do not silently replace those of the first.
- Close politely or request a useful detail.
  - Prašau parašyti laiką. Ačiū!
  - Give one useful detail at a time. A polite request can close a brief practical message.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A1-shopping:confirm

Question purpose: Four euros?

Prepared question: Keturi eurai?

Partner message: Keturi eurai.

Detail check: Which total did your partner clarify?

Key: Four euros

Optional response frame: Keturi eurai.

Response goals:
- Restate the price, keeping the amount and currency distinct from the item quantity.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-shopping:correct

Question purpose: Three euros?

Prepared question: Trys eurai?

Partner message: Keturi eurai. Mokėsiu grynaisiais.

Detail check: Which total did your partner clarify?

Key: Four euros

Optional response frame: Keturi eurai.

Response goals:
- Restate the price, keeping the amount and currency distinct from the item quantity.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-travel:confirm

Question purpose: To the left?

Prepared question: Į kairę?

Partner message: Sukite į kairę.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Sukite į kairę.

Response goals:
- Restate the clarified turn so the visitor can follow the correct direction.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A1-travel:correct

Question purpose: To the right?

Prepared question: Į dešinę?

Partner message: Eikite tiesiai, paskui sukite į kairę.

Detail check: Which turn did your partner clarify?

Key: Left

Optional response frame: Sukite į kairę.

<a id="a2"></a>
## A2 · Change an everyday plan

Unit reference: A2

### Learning goal

Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence.

Builds on: A1-capstone

### Pattern explanation

Use pirmiausia and paskui to order events, bet for a contrast and nes for a reason. Negaliu says cannot; gal galime proposes a replacement.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- vakar - yesterday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-0.d49ce5b79c30.mp3)
- pirmiausia - first [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-1.78125c16254c.mp3)
- paskui - then / afterwards [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-2.f41902a0f1da.mp3)
- pavargusi - tired (female speaker) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-3.90714f04c0e5.mp3)
- rytoj - tomorrow [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A1.word-7.d0cca6c75219.mp3)
- penktadienį - on Friday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-5.6d8d6dffcfff.mp3)
- nes - because [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-6.292dc7d95822.mp3)
- lyti - to rain [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-7.4c7d46aaa5d3.mp3)

### Additional source language

- aštuntos — eight, in iki aštuntos valandos: until eight
  - Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos, todėl septintą ateiti negaliu.
- darysime — we will do
  - O ką darysime, jei lis?
- dirbti — to work
  - Rytoj negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti.
- dėl — about / because of, with the following noun form
  - Skambinu dėl mūsų susitikimo.
- galėtume — we could
  - Galėtume pasivaikščioti ir išgerti arbatos.
- išgerti — have a drink / drink
  - Galėtume pasivaikščioti ir išgerti arbatos.
- jei — if
  - Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.
- kada — when
  - Kada galėtume susitikti?
- kavinėje — in a café
  - Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.
- kodėl — why
  - Kodėl penktadienį negali ateiti?
- ką — what, object form
  - O ką darysime, jei lis?
- laikas — time
  - Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.
- lis — it will rain
  - Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.
- mūsų — our
  - Skambinu dėl mūsų susitikimo.
- negali — you cannot, informal singular
  - Kodėl penktadienį negali ateiti?
- parašyk — write / let me know, informal singular instruction
  - Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.
- pasivaikščioti — take a walk
  - Galėtume pasivaikščioti ir išgerti arbatos.
- septinta — seven o’clock, in septinta valanda
  - Man tinka septinta valanda.
- siūlau — I suggest
  - Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko.
- skambinu — I am calling
  - Skambinu dėl mūsų susitikimo.
- susitikimo — meeting, form after dėl
  - Skambinu dėl mūsų susitikimo.
- tau — to / for you, informal singular
  - Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.
- tinka — works / suits
  - Man tinka septinta valanda.
- todėl — so / therefore
  - Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos, todėl septintą ateiti negaliu.
- turiu — I have; turiu dirbti means I have to work
  - Rytoj negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti.
- valanda — hour; also the clock-hour word in septinta valanda
  - Man tinka septinta valanda.
- valandos — hour, form in iki aštuntos valandos
  - Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos, todėl septintą ateiti negaliu.
- šeštadienis — Saturday
  - Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.
- šeštadienį — on Saturday
  - Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį?

### Sentence-pattern preparation

- Vakar buvau mieste. — Yesterday I was in town.
- Pirmiausia nuėjau į parką. — First I went to the park.
- Paskui susitikau su drauge. — Then I met a friend.
- Buvo smagu, bet pradėjo lyti. — It was fun, but it started raining.
- Grįžau namo, nes buvau pavargusi. — I went home because I was tired (female speaker).
- Rytoj negaliu ateiti. — I cannot come tomorrow.
- Gal galime susitikti penktadienį? — Perhaps we can meet on Friday?
- Ačiū už supratimą. — Thank you for understanding.

### Teaching notes

- Use simple connectors to show a real relationship: addition, contrast, reason or sequence.
- A practical message needs both the problem and a clear proposed next step.
- For a past event, keep the order easy to follow. You do not need a long sentence to explain a reason.

### Reading source

Labas, Lina! Vakar buvau mieste. Pirmiausia nuėjau į parką, o paskui susitikau su drauge. Buvo smagu, bet pradėjo lyti. Grįžau namo, nes buvau pavargusi.

Rytoj negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti. Gal galime susitikti penktadienį? Man tinka septinta valanda. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas. Ačiū už supratimą!

English guide:
Hello, Lina/Ana! Yesterday I was in town. First I went to the park, then I met a friend. It was fun, but it started raining. I went home because I was tired.

I cannot come tomorrow because I have to work. Could we meet on Friday? Seven works for me. Write and say whether that time works for you. Thank you for understanding!

### Listening source

Labas, Lina. Skambinu dėl mūsų susitikimo. Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos, todėl septintą ateiti negaliu. Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį? Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko. Galėtume pasivaikščioti ir išgerti arbatos. Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis. Ačiū!

English guide:
The caller must work until eight on Friday and cannot come at seven. They propose Saturday at ten beside the park, for a walk and tea. If it rains, they suggest the café next to the park. They ask the listener to confirm Saturday.

[Full listening recording](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.listen.38e68df3994b.mp3)

### Reading task key

1. Why does the writer change the plan?
   - She has to work **[key]**
   - She dislikes her friend
2. What should the recipient do?
   - Confirm whether the new time works **[key]**
   - Buy train tickets
3. How does the writer describe the outing?
   - They stayed home all day
   - It was fun before the rain began **[key]**
   - They disliked meeting their friend

### Listening task key

1. Why does the Friday plan change?
   - The caller is ill
   - The caller has to work **[key]**
   - The park is closed
2. What replacement is suggested?
   - Friday at ten
   - Saturday at eight
   - Saturday at ten **[key]**
3. What happens if it rains?
   - They meet in the nearby café **[key]**
   - They cancel every plan
   - They meet at the workplace

### Spoken production

Describe a real or invented day out, explain a practical problem and suggest a new arrangement.

- Put events in a clear order.
- Explain why the original plan changed.
- Suggest a day and time, and ask for confirmation.

### Recorded partner role-play

1. Kodėl penktadienį negali ateiti?
   - Meaning: Why can you not come on Friday?
   - Response goal: Explain a practical reason.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.turn-0.cc22be319ae7.mp3)
2. Kada galėtume susitikti?
   - Meaning: When could we meet?
   - Response goal: Suggest another day and time.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.turn-1.0629c040f845.mp3)
3. O ką darysime, jei lis?
   - Meaning: And what will we do if it rains?
   - Response goal: Offer a practical alternative.
   - [Partner audio](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.turn-2.3e6393cc0259.mp3)

### Spoken mediation

Leave a short voice message explaining the two different changed plans in the reading and listening. Do not confuse Friday’s written proposal with Saturday’s spoken proposal.

### Writing assignments

#### Tell a friend about a day out

Reader: A friend

Describe a recent real or invented outing.

Suggested length: 20–70 words.

- Put the events in order.
- Say what you liked or disliked.
- Explain one reason.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Vakar buvau mieste. Pirmiausia nuėjau į parką, o paskui susitikau su drauge. Buvo smagu, bet pradėjo lyti. Grįžau namo, nes buvau pavargusi.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Put the events in order
  - Vakar buvau mieste. Pirmiausia nuėjau į parką, o paskui susitikau su drauge.
  - The time and sequence words show what happened first and next.
- Say what you liked or disliked
  - Buvo smagu, bet pradėjo lyti.
  - This gives a reaction and a contrasting complication.
- Explain one reason
  - Grįžau namo, nes buvau pavargusi.
  - Nes / porque links the action to its reason, rather than just listing another event.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### Change an arrangement

Reader: A friend or classmate

You cannot attend an agreed meeting. Send a useful message.

Suggested length: 15–70 words.

- Explain why you cannot attend.
- Suggest another day or time.
- Ask for confirmation.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Penktadienį negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti. Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį dešimtą valandą prie parko? Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Explain why you cannot attend
  - Penktadienį negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti.
  - The day, problem and reason are connected in one practical explanation.
- Propose another arrangement
  - Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį dešimtą valandą prie parko?
  - A useful alternative gives a day, time and place; it is a proposal, not a confirmed booking.
- Ask for confirmation
  - Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.
  - This asks the other person to confirm whether the replacement works.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.

#### Recommend a familiar place

Reader: A visitor

Describe a place you know or invent a simple place.

Suggested length: 15–70 words.

- Say what someone can do there.
- Give a reason to visit.
- Mention a practical detail or limitation.


##### Project-specific preparation

Connect the useful details: explain a reason, make the change clear and give the reader a next step. The suggested length is practice guidance, not a CEFR requirement.

Worked model:
Kavinėje šalia parko galime išgerti arbatos. Siūlau susitikti prie parko, nes galime pasivaikščioti. Man tinka septinta valanda.

Purpose practice (correct line and explanation):

- Say what someone can do there
  - Kavinėje šalia parko galime išgerti arbatos.
  - This identifies the place and a simple activity available there.
- Give a reason to visit
  - Siūlau susitikti prie parko, nes galime pasivaikščioti.
  - The reason makes the proposal useful for the visitor instead of only naming a place.
- Give a practical detail
  - Man tinka septinta valanda.
  - A convenient meeting time is a practical detail. It is not a claim about opening hours.

Learners can save their own planning details with the draft. Purpose practice does not complete or grade the project.


### Controlled reading transfer

A message proposing another arrangement

Changed combinations of prepared language. Feedback is held to the end. Repeated source versions are labelled as repeat practice; this does not establish a CEFR level.

#### saturday-eight-rain

Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos, todėl septintą ateiti negaliu. Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį aštuntą valandą prie parko? Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.

- Why is Friday at seven not possible?
  - Model detail: Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos, todėl septintą ateiti negaliu.
  - The writer works until eight **[key]**
  - The park closes at seven
  - The friend has already cancelled Saturday
  - Explanation: The reason is the writer’s work, not a closure or a reply from the friend.
- Which replacement is proposed in this message?
  - Model detail: Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį aštuntą valandą prie parko?
  - Saturday at ten
  - Friday at eight
  - Saturday at eight **[key]**
  - Explanation: Read this proposal rather than carrying over the ten o’clock time from the prepared message.
- What changes if it rains?
  - Model detail: Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.
  - The meeting is automatically cancelled
  - They meet in the café beside the park **[key]**
  - The meeting moves back to Friday
  - Explanation: The condition changes the meeting place; it does not cancel the arrangement or change its day.

#### friday-ten-reply

Rytoj negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti. Gal galime susitikti penktadienį dešimtą valandą? Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas. Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje.

- What explains the proposed change?
  - Model detail: Rytoj negaliu ateiti, nes turiu dirbti.
  - The café has confirmed a booking
  - The writer has to work tomorrow **[key]**
  - The friend refuses every meeting
  - Explanation: Nes introduces the reason for not coming tomorrow.
- What should the reader do next?
  - Model detail: Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.
  - Reply whether the proposed time works **[key]**
  - Assume ten has already been agreed
  - Ask the writer to work until ten
  - Explanation: The writer requests a response about the proposed time.
- What can you conclude about Friday at ten?
  - Model detail: Gal galime susitikti penktadienį dešimtą valandą? Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.
  - Both people have already confirmed it
  - It has been cancelled because of rain
  - It is proposed, with a reply still requested **[key]**
  - Explanation: A proposal and request for a reply do not establish that the other person has accepted.

### Controlled listening transfer

A voice message about an arrangement or a day out

The learner hears the complete recording before answering. The transcript below appears only after all three answers in the learner interface. First attempts and repeats are tracked separately.

#### friday-ten-park

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/lt.A2.friday-ten-park.11da1143ae34.mp3)

Labas, Lina. Skambinu dėl mūsų susitikimo. Penktadienį turiu dirbti iki aštuntos valandos. Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko. Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas. Ačiū už supratimą.

- Which meeting time does the speaker propose?
  - Model detail: Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko.
  - Eight
  - Ten **[key]**
  - Seven
  - Explanation: Eight is when work ends. Ten is the proposed meeting time.
- What happens if it rains?
  - Model detail: Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.
  - The friend must work later
  - The meeting is automatically cancelled
  - They meet in the café beside the park **[key]**
  - Explanation: The rain condition changes the place, not the time or the need to reply.
- What does the speaker still need from the listener?
  - Model detail: Parašyk, ar tau tinka šis laikas.
  - A reply about whether the time works **[key]**
  - Proof that the park is open
  - A confirmation that the meeting already happened
  - Explanation: The message asks for a reply; it does not establish that the listener has accepted.

#### outing-tired-home

[Complete recording](../../../audio/listening-transfer/lt.A2.outing-tired-home.ea176eea3e7d.mp3)

Labas! Vakar buvau mieste. Pirmiausia nuėjau į parką. Paskui susitikau su drauge. Buvo smagu, bet pradėjo lyti. Grįžau namo, nes buvau pavargusi. Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį? Siūlau septintą valandą prie parko.

- What happened before meeting the friend?
  - Model detail: Pirmiausia nuėjau į parką. Paskui susitikau su drauge.
  - The speaker went home
  - The speaker went to the park **[key]**
  - The speaker worked until eight
  - Explanation: Pirmiausia and paskui give the order of the two events.
- What reason does the speaker give for going home?
  - Model detail: Grįžau namo, nes buvau pavargusi.
  - The friend cancelled Saturday
  - The café was closed
  - The speaker was tired **[key]**
  - Explanation: Rain is mentioned, but the stated reason for returning home is tiredness.
- Which new arrangement is proposed?
  - Model detail: Gal galime susitikti šeštadienį? Siūlau septintą valandą prie parko.
  - Saturday at seven **[key]**
  - Friday at ten
  - Saturday at eight
  - Explanation: The proposed Saturday meeting is separate from the account of yesterday.

### Required written exchanges

Write the question, understand the prepared partner reply, then draft and revise a response. Own writing is self-reviewed; no automatic accuracy or CEFR grade is assigned.

#### A2:time

Question purpose: When could we meet?

Prepared question: Kada galėtume susitikti?

Partner message: Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.

Detail check: What arrangement does your partner propose?

Key: Saturday at ten near the park

Optional response frame: Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge Saturday at ten and say whether it works for you, or propose a prepared alternative.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

#### A2:rain

Question purpose: And what will we do if it rains?

Prepared question: O ką darysime, jei lis?

Partner message: Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.

Detail check: What does rain change?

Key: The meeting place becomes the café beside the park

Optional response frame: Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.

Response goals:
- Acknowledge the rain plan and restate the café location without changing the day or time.
- Preserve the confirmed detail and any condition in the partner’s message.
- Close or continue the exchange in a way that fits the recipient.

### Guided checking exchange

Ask about the replacement time or the rain condition, then make a useful response.

Reuses the prepared listening source. The learner selects the question they intend to say; that selection drives a recorded branch. Both own responses require recording, replay and self-review. Speech is not automatically interpreted or graded.

- Checking question: Kada galėtume susitikti?
  - Meaning: When could we meet?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A2.question-time.0629c040f845.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A2.time.2235687aa934.mp3)

- Checking question: O ką darysime, jei lis?
  - Meaning: And what will we do if it rains?
  - [Question model](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A2.question-rain.3e6393cc0259.mp3)
  - Partner reply: Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.
  - [Recorded partner](../../../audio/interaction-practice/lt.A2.rain.fe428ce06fb2.mp3)

B1–C2 question frames are optional and their use is saved. Intention selection drives the partner; speech is never interpreted automatically.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: time

What arrangement does your partner propose?

Key: Saturday at ten near the park

Own-response goal: Acknowledge Saturday at ten and say whether it works for you, or propose a prepared alternative.

Prepared wording: Siūlau dešimtą valandą prie parko. Parašyk, ar tau tinka šeštadienis.

#### Reply-specific follow-up: rain

What does rain change?

Key: The meeting place becomes the café beside the park

Own-response goal: Acknowledge the rain plan and restate the café location without changing the day or time.

Prepared wording: Jei lis, susitikime kavinėje šalia parko.

<a id="a2-services"></a>
## A2 · Arrange and change an appointment

Unit reference: A2-services

### Learning goal

Follow opening times, explain a booking problem and agree a replacement.

Builds on: A2

### Pattern explanation

Norėčiau makes a polite request. Galiu gives availability. Tas laikas netinka declines a particular time, so follow it with an alternative rather than treating the whole appointment as cancelled.

### Support plan

Study the model, then make brief notes. Retell the arrangement without reading whole sentences; use the guide again when needed.

### Skill targets

- reading: Locate routine information and distinguish a plan from a change to it. (selected scale: Overall reading comprehension)
- listening: Follow the essential details of a clear everyday message. (selected scale: Overall oral comprehension)
- production: Describe familiar events and explain a practical change in a short sequence. (selected scale: Overall oral production)
- interaction: Exchange routine information, propose alternatives and ask for confirmation. (selected scale: Overall oral interaction)
- mediation: Pass on the key details of an everyday arrangement to someone else. (selected scale: Relaying specific information in speech or sign)
- writing: Link simple sentences to explain an arrangement, preference or change. (selected scale: Overall written production / Overall written interaction)

### Vocabulary in context

- užsiregistruoti - to register / make an appointment [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-0.1d1ef38066ab.mp3)
- dokumentus - documents (object form) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-1.29cde06996bf.mp3)
- antradienį - on Tuesday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-2.468315d7f4cb.mp3)
- penktadienį - on Friday [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2.word-5.6d8d6dffcfff.mp3)
- anksčiau - earlier [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-4.c9b114a158d5.mp3)
- patvirtinti - to confirm [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-5.6bf4f8f86d43.mp3)
- laikas - time / appointment slot [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-6.e824fd8dd3be.mp3)
- ketvirtadienio - Thursday (form after iki) [Hear](../../../audio/skills/lt.A2-services.word-7.a4c8f5eb6a1d.mp3)

### Additional source language
